+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", worldSize);
+        std::printf("OpenMP threads per rank: %d\n", omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("CUDA devices per node: %d\n", deviceCount);
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> localA(localElements);
+    std::vector<double> B(matrixElements);
+    initMatrixRows(localA, N, firstRow, localRows);
+
+    // B is identical on every rank.  Only rank zero initializes it; the
+    // chunked broadcast also supports matrices larger than MPI's int count.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        initMatrix(B, N);
+    }
+    broadcastDoubles(B.data(), matrixElements, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;
+    const std::size_t safeLocalElements = std::max<std::size_t>(localElements, 1);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA),
+                          safeLocalElements * sizeof(double)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB),
+                          matrixElements * sizeof(double)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC),
+                          safeLocalElements * sizeof(double)));
+
+    if (localElements > 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, localA.data(),
+                              localElements * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceB, B.data(),
+                          matrixElements * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+
+    std::vector<double> localC(localElements);
+    const bool needFullResult = validate || printResults;
+    std::vector<double> C;
+    if (rank == 0 && needFullResult) {
+        C.resize(matrixElements);
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    if (localRows > 0) {
+        const dim3 block(static_cast<unsigned int>(kTileColumns),
+                         static_cast<unsigned int>(kTileRows), 1);
+        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((N + kTileColumns - 1) / kTileColumns),
+                        static_cast<unsigned int>((localRows + kTileRows - 1) / kTileRows),
+                        1);
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<<<grid, block>>>(deviceA, deviceB, deviceC,
+                                              static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                              static_cast<int>(N));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+
+    if (localElements > 0) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC,
+                              localElements * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (needFullResult) {
+        if (localElements > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            abortWithMessage(rank, "local matrix result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count capacity");
+        }
+
+        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize));
+            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
+                std::size_t sourceFirstRow = 0;
+                std::size_t sourceRows = 0;
+                calculateRowPartition(N, worldSize, source,
+                                      sourceFirstRow, sourceRows);
+                const std::size_t sourceElements = sourceRows * N;
+                const std::size_t sourceDisplacement = sourceFirstRow * N;
+                if (sourceElements > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+                    sourceDisplacement > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                    abortWithMessage(rank, "matrix is too large for MPI_Gatherv metadata");
+                }
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    static_cast<int>(sourceElements);
+                displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    static_cast<int>(sourceDisplacement);
+            }
         }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
     }
-    
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceA));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceB));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceC));
+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long durationMilliseconds = std::max<long>(
+            1L, static_cast<long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0)));
+        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
+
+        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                               static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                              (elapsed > 0.0 ? elapsed : 1e-12) / 1e9;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            std::vector<double> validationA(matrixElements);
+            initMatrix(validationA, N);
+            const bool valid = validateResult(validationA, B, C, N);
+            if (valid) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                MPI_Finalize();
+                return 1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;
 }

+constexpr int kTileRows = 8;

+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept

+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const std::size_t N,
+                                    const std::size_t i,

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
+                    const std::size_t N,
+                    const std::size_t firstRow,
+                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
+    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
+    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
+    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
+         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
+        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
+    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
+}
+
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const int rows,
+                                     const int N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
+    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
+        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
+                             : 0.0;
+
+        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
+        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
+        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
+            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
+            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
+                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
+                                  : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
+            if (k < validK) {
+                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && column < N) {
+        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
     }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
+               const char* expression,
+               const int rank) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
+                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
+    }
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)
+
+void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
+                      const std::size_t count,
+                      const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
+        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseArguments(const int argc,
+                    char** argv,
+                    std::size_t& N,
+                    bool& validate,
+                    bool& printResults,
+                    bool& showHelp) {
+    N = 512;
+    validate = false;
+    printResults = false;
+    showHelp = false;
+
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            errno = 0;
+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const unsigned long long parsed =
+                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
+            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
+            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
+                return false;
+            }
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            showHelp = true;
+        } else {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
+    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
+    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
+    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}
+
+bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                   const std::vector<double>& B,
+                   const std::vector<double>& C,

+        for (std::size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
+            const std::size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
+            const std::size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
+
             double expected = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                 expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
             }
-            
+
             const double actual = C[i * N + j];
             const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
-            
+
             if (relError > 1e-6) {
-                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
+                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
+                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                 return false;
             }
         }
     }
-    
+
     return true;
 }

+void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
+                           const int worldSize,
+                           const int rank,
+                           std::size_t& firstRow,
+                           std::size_t& rowCount) {
+    const std::size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    const std::size_t remainder = N % static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    firstRow = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
+               std::min(static_cast<std::size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    rowCount = baseRows + (static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);

-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");

+    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;

+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", worldSize);

+    // chunked broadcast also supports matrices larger than MPI's int count.

+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;

+                          safeLocalElements * sizeof(double)));

+                          matrixElements * sizeof(double)));

+                        static_cast<unsigned int>((localRows + kTileRows - 1) / kTileRows),

+                                              static_cast<int>(localRows),

+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+

+            abortWithMessage(rank, "local matrix result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count capacity");

+                                      sourceFirstRow, sourceRows);

+                const std::size_t sourceDisplacement = sourceFirstRow * N;
+                if (sourceElements > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+                    sourceDisplacement > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                    abortWithMessage(rank, "matrix is too large for MPI_Gatherv metadata");
+                }
+                receiveCounts[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    static_cast<int>(sourceElements);
+                displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
+                    static_cast<int>(sourceDisplacement);
+            }
         }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
     }

+    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long durationMilliseconds = std::max<long>(
+            1L, static_cast<long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0)));
+        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
+
+        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                               static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
+                              (elapsed > 0.0 ? elapsed : 1e-12) / 1e9;
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            std::vector<double> validationA(matrixElements);
+            initMatrix(validationA, N);

+            if (valid) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                MPI_Finalize();
+                return 1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
     return 0;
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
+                    const std::size_t N,
+                    const std::size_t firstRow,
+                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
+    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
+    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
+    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
+         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
+        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
+    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
+}
+
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const int rows,
+                                     const int N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
+    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
+        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
+                             : 0.0;
+
+        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
+        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
+        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
+            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
+            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
+                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
+                                  : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
+            if (k < validK) {
+                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && column < N) {
+        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
     }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
+               const char* expression,
+               const int rank) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
+                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
+    }
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)

+void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
+                      const std::size_t count,
+                      const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
+        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseArguments(const int argc,
+                    char** argv,
+                    std::size_t& N,
+                    bool& validate,
+                    bool& printResults,
+                    bool& showHelp) {
+    N = 512;
+    validate = false;
+    printResults = false;
+    showHelp = false;
+
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            errno = 0;
+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const unsigned long long parsed =
+                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
+            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
+            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
+                return false;
+            }
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            showHelp = true;
+        } else {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
+    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
+    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
+    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");

+
     return true;

+void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
+                           const int worldSize,
+                           const int rank,
+                           std::size_t& firstRow,
+                           std::size_t& rowCount) {
+    const std::size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    const std::size_t remainder = N % static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    firstRow = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
+               std::min(static_cast<std::size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    rowCount = baseRows + (static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);

+                    rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr,

+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
+                    const std::size_t N,
+                    const std::size_t firstRow,
+                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
+    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
+    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
+    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
+         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
+        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
+    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
+}
+
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const int rows,
+                                     const int N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
+    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
+        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
+                             : 0.0;
+
+        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
+        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
+        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
+            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
+            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
+                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
+                                  : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
+            if (k < validK) {
+                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && column < N) {
+        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
     }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
+               const char* expression,
+               const int rank) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
+                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
+    }
+}

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)
+
+void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
+                      const std::size_t count,
+                      const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
+        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseArguments(const int argc,
+                    char** argv,
+                    std::size_t& N,
+                    bool& validate,
+                    bool& printResults,
+                    bool& showHelp) {
+    N = 512;
+    validate = false;
+    printResults = false;
+    showHelp = false;
+
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            errno = 0;
+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const unsigned long long parsed =
+                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
+            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
+            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
+                return false;
+            }
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            showHelp = true;
+        } else {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
+    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
+    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
+    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}

-                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);

+                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                 return false;
             }
         }
     }
-    
+
     return true;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
+                           const int worldSize,
+                           const int rank,
+                           std::size_t& firstRow,
+                           std::size_t& rowCount) {
+    const std::size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    const std::size_t remainder = N % static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    firstRow = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
+               std::min(static_cast<std::size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    rowCount = baseRows + (static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
+                    const std::size_t N,
+                    const std::size_t firstRow,
+                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
+    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
+    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
+    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
+         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
+        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
+    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
+}
+
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const int rows,
+                                     const int N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
+    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
+        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
+                             : 0.0;
+
+        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
+        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
+        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
+            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
+            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
+                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
+                                  : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
+            if (k < validK) {
+                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && column < N) {
+        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
     }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
+               const char* expression,
+               const int rank) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
+                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
+    }

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)

+void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
+                      const std::size_t count,
+                      const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
+        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseArguments(const int argc,
+                    char** argv,
+                    std::size_t& N,
+                    bool& validate,
+                    bool& printResults,
+                    bool& showHelp) {
+    N = 512;
+    validate = false;
+    printResults = false;
+    showHelp = false;
+
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            errno = 0;
+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const unsigned long long parsed =
+                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
+            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
+            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
+                return false;
+            }
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            showHelp = true;
+        } else {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
+    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
+    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
+    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}

+            const std::size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
+            const std::size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
+
             double expected = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                 expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
             }
-            
+
             const double actual = C[i * N + j];
             const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
-            
+
             if (relError > 1e-6) {
-                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
+                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
+                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                 return false;
             }
         }
     }
-    
+
     return true;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
+                           const int worldSize,
+                           const int rank,
+                           std::size_t& firstRow,
+                           std::size_t& rowCount) {
+    const std::size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    const std::size_t remainder = N % static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    firstRow = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
+               std::min(static_cast<std::size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    rowCount = baseRows + (static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
+                    const std::size_t N,
+                    const std::size_t firstRow,
+                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
+    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
+    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
+    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
+         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
+        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
+    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
+}
+
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const int rows,
+                                     const int N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
+    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
+        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
+                             : 0.0;
+
+        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
+        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
+        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
+            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
+            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
+                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
+                                  : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
+            if (k < validK) {
+                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && column < N) {
+        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
     }
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
+               const char* expression,
+               const int rank) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
+                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)
+
+void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
+                      const std::size_t count,
+                      const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
+        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseArguments(const int argc,
+                    char** argv,
+                    std::size_t& N,
+                    bool& validate,
+                    bool& printResults,
+                    bool& showHelp) {
+    N = 512;
+    validate = false;
+    printResults = false;
+    showHelp = false;
+
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            errno = 0;
+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const unsigned long long parsed =
+                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
+            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
+            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
+                return false;
+            }
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            showHelp = true;
+        } else {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
+    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
+    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
+    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}
+

+            const std::size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
+
             double expected = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                 expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
             }
-            
+
             const double actual = C[i * N + j];
             const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
-            
+
             if (relError > 1e-6) {
-                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
-                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
+                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
+                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                 return false;
             }
         }
     }
-    
+
     return true;
 }
 
-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
+                           const int worldSize,
+                           const int rank,

+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
+                    const std::size_t N,
+                    const std::size_t firstRow,
+                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
+    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
+    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
+    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
+    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
+         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
+         ++localRow) {
+        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
+        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
+        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
+    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
+}
+
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     const int rows,
+                                     const int N) {
+    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
+    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
+
+    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
+    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
+    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
+
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
+        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
+        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
+                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
+                             : 0.0;
+
+        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
+        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
+        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
+            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
+            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
+                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
+                                  : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
+            if (k < validK) {
+                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (row < rows && column < N) {
+        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
     }
 }

-            
+[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
+               const char* expression,
+               const int rank) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        char message[512];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
+                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)
+
+void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
+                      const std::size_t count,
+                      const int root,
+                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
+        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
+        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+bool parseArguments(const int argc,
+                    char** argv,
+                    std::size_t& N,
+                    bool& validate,
+                    bool& printResults,
+                    bool& showHelp) {
+    N = 512;
+    validate = false;
+    printResults = false;
+    showHelp = false;
+
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            errno = 0;
+            char* end = nullptr;
+            const unsigned long long parsed =
+                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
+            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
+            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
+                return false;
+            }
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            showHelp = true;
+        } else {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
+    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
+    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
+    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}
+
+bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                   const std::vector<double>& B,
+                   const std::vector<double>& C,
+                   const std::size_t N) {
+    constexpr std::size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
+
+    for (std::size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
+        for (std::size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
+            const std::size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
+            const std::size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
+
             double expected = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
+            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                 expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
             }
-            
+
             const double actual = C[i * N + j];
             const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
-

+void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
+                           const int worldSize,
+                           const int rank,
+                           std::size_t& firstRow,
+                           std::size_t& rowCount) {
+    const std::size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    const std::size_t remainder = N % static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
+    firstRow = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
+               std::min(static_cast<std::size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    rowCount = baseRows + (static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
 }

    20	namespace {
    21	
    22	constexpr int kTileColumns = 32;
    23	constexpr int kTileRows = 8;
    24	
    25	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
    26	// identical to the original implementation so every MPI rank can generate
    27	// its own rows without communicating matrix A.
    28	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const std::size_t N,
    29	                                    const std::size_t i,
    30	                                    const std::size_t j) noexcept {
    31	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    32	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    33	}
    34	
    35	void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat,
    36	                    const std::size_t N,
    37	                    const std::size_t firstRow,
    38	                    const std::size_t rowCount) {
    39	    // Matrix initialization is deliberately parallel even though the actual
    40	    // multiplication is performed by CUDA.  It avoids serial host-side setup
    41	    // becoming visible at scale and honors the user's OpenMP configuration.
    42	    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    43	    for (std::int64_t localRow = 0;
    44	         localRow < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rowCount);
    45	         ++localRow) {
    46	        const std::size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow);
    47	        const std::size_t rowStart = static_cast<std::size_t>(localRow) * N;
    48	        for (std::size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
    49	            mat[rowStart + column] = getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
    50	        }
    51	    }
    52	}
    53	
    54	void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const std::size_t N) {
    55	    initMatrixRows(mat, N, 0, N);
    56	}
    57	
    58	__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
    59	                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
    60	                                     double* __restrict__ C,
    61	                                     const int rows,
    62	                                     const int N) {
    63	    __shared__ double tileA[kTileRows][kTileColumns];
    64	    __shared__ double tileB[kTileColumns][kTileColumns];
    65	
    66	    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
    67	    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);
    68	    const int row = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * kTileRows + ty;
    69	    const int column = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * kTileColumns + tx;
    70	
    71	    double sum = 0.0;
    72	    for (int tileStart = 0; tileStart < N; tileStart += kTileColumns) {
    73	        const int aColumn = tileStart + tx;
    74	        tileA[ty][tx] = (row < rows && aColumn < N)
    75	                             ? A[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + aColumn]
    76	                             : 0.0;
    77	
    78	        // There are four B rows for each of the eight thread rows.  This
    79	        // fills the 32x32 B tile with coalesced accesses using a 32x8 block.
    80	        for (int bRow = ty; bRow < kTileColumns; bRow += kTileRows) {
    81	            const int bRowInMatrix = tileStart + bRow;
    82	            tileB[bRow][tx] = (bRowInMatrix < N && column < N)
    83	                                  ? B[static_cast<std::size_t>(bRowInMatrix) * N + column]
    84	                                  : 0.0;
    85	        }
    86	        __syncthreads();
    87	
    88	        const int validK = min(kTileColumns, N - tileStart);
    89	        #pragma unroll
    90	        for (int k = 0; k < kTileColumns; ++k) {
    91	            if (k < validK) {
    92	                sum += tileA[ty][k] * tileB[k][tx];
    93	            }
    94	        }
    95	        __syncthreads();
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    if (row < rows && column < N) {
    99	        C[static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * N + column] = sum;
   100	    }
   101	}
   102	
   103	[[noreturn]] void abortWithMessage(const int rank, const char* message) {
   104	    std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI rank %d: %s\n", rank, message);
   105	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   106	    std::abort();
   107	}
   108	
   109	void checkCuda(const cudaError_t error,
   110	               const char* expression,
   111	               const int rank) {
   112	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
   113	        char message[512];
   114	        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "CUDA error in %s: %s",
   115	                      expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
   116	        abortWithMessage(rank, message);
   117	    }
   118	}
   119	
   120	#define CUDA_CHECK(call) checkCuda((call), #call, rank)
   121	
   122	void broadcastDoubles(double* data,
   123	                      const std::size_t count,
   124	                      const int root,
   125	                      const MPI_Comm communicator) {
   126	    constexpr std::size_t maxMpiCount =
   127	        static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   128	    for (std::size_t offset = 0; offset < count;) {
   129	        const std::size_t remaining = count - offset;
   130	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(remaining, maxMpiCount));
   131	        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, MPI_DOUBLE, root, communicator);
   132	        offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(chunk);
   133	    }
   134	}
   135	
   136	bool parseArguments(const int argc,
   137	                    char** argv,
   138	                    std::size_t& N,
   139	                    bool& validate,
   140	                    bool& printResults,
   141	                    bool& showHelp) {
   142	    N = 512;
   143	    validate = false;
   144	    printResults = false;
   145	    showHelp = false;
   146	
   147	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   148	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   149	            errno = 0;
   150	            char* end = nullptr;
   151	            const unsigned long long parsed =
   152	                std::strtoull(argv[++i], &end, 10);
   153	            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0) {
   154	                return false;
   155	            }
   156	            N = static_cast<std::size_t>(parsed);
   157	            if (static_cast<unsigned long long>(N) != parsed) {
   158	                return false;
   159	            }
   160	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   161	            validate = true;
   162	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   163	            printResults = true;
   164	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   165	            showHelp = true;
   166	        } else {
   167	            return false;
   168	        }
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    // The CUDA kernel and the MPI count/displacement metadata use int-sized
   172	    // dimensions.  Values beyond this are not representable by one matrix in
   173	    // this benchmark and are rejected before any allocation occurs.
   174	    if (N > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   175	        return false;
   176	    }
   177	    if (N > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / N) {
   178	        return false;
   179	    }
   180	    if ((N * N) > std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
   181	        return false;
   182	    }
   183	    return true;
   184	}
   185	
   186	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   187	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   188	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   189	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   190	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   191	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   192	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   193	}
   194	
   195	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A,
   196	                   const std::vector<double>& B,
   197	                   const std::vector<double>& C,
   198	                   const std::size_t N) {
   199	    constexpr std::size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   200	
   201	    for (std::size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   202	        for (std::size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   203	            const std::size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
   204	            const std::size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
   205	
   206	            double expected = 0.0;
   207	            for (std::size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   208	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   209	            }
   210	
   211	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   212	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   213	
   214	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   215	                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   216	                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   217	                return false;
   218	            }
   219	        }
   220	    }
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    return true;
   223	}
   224	
   225	void calculateRowPartition(const std::size_t N,
   226	                           const int worldSize,
   227	                           const int rank,
   228	                           std::size_t& firstRow,
   229	                           std::size_t& rowCount) {
   230	    const std::size_t baseRows = N / static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
   231	    const std::size_t remainder = N % static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize);
   232	    firstRow = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) * baseRows +
   233	               std::min(static_cast<std::size_t>(rank), remainder);
   234	    rowCount = baseRows + (static_cast<std::size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   235	}
   236	
   237	} // namespace
   238	
   239	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   240	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   241	    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &providedThreadLevel);
   242	
   243	    int rank = 0;
   244	    int worldSize = 1;
   245	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   246	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   247	
   248	    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   249	        abortWithMessage(rank, "MPI implementation does not provide MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED");
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    std::size_t N = 512;
   253	    bool validate = false;
   254	    bool printResults = false;
   255	    bool showHelp = false;
   256	    int argumentStatus = 1;
   257	
   258	    if (rank == 0) {
   259	        argumentStatus = parseArguments(argc, argv, N, validate, printResults, showHelp) ? 0 : 1;
   260	    }
   261	
   262	    unsigned long long wireN = static_cast<unsigned long long>(N);
   263	    int wireValidate = validate ? 1 : 0;
   264	    int wirePrintResults = printResults ? 1 : 0;
   265	    int wireShowHelp = showHelp ? 1 : 0;
   266	    MPI_Bcast(&argumentStatus, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   267	    MPI_Bcast(&wireN, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   268	    MPI_Bcast(&wireValidate, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   269	    MPI_Bcast(&wirePrintResults, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   270	    MPI_Bcast(&wireShowHelp, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   271	
   272	    if (argumentStatus != 0) {
   273	        if (rank == 0) {
   274	            std::printf("Invalid command line arguments.\n");
   275	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   276	        }
   277	        MPI_Finalize();
   278	        return 1;
   279	    }
   280	    N = static_cast<std::size_t>(wireN);
   281	    validate = wireValidate != 0;
   282	    printResults = wirePrintResults != 0;
   283	    showHelp = wireShowHelp != 0;
   284	
   285	    if (showHelp) {
   286	        if (rank == 0) {
   287	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   288	        }
   289	        MPI_Finalize();
   290	        return 0;
   291	    }
   292	
   293	    const std::size_t matrixElements = N * N;
   294	    std::size_t firstRow = 0;
   295	    std::size_t localRows = 0;
   296	    calculateRowPartition(N, worldSize, rank, firstRow, localRows);
   297	    const std::size_t localElements = localRows * N;
   298	
   299	    // Select one GPU per local MPI rank.  MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED makes the
   300	    // mapping restart at zero on each node, which is the required mapping for
   301	    // multi-node accelerator jobs.
   302	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   303	    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,
   304	                        MPI_INFO_NULL, &localCommunicator);
   305	    int localRank = 0;
   306	    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
   307	
   308	    int deviceCount = 0;
   309	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
   310	    if (deviceCount <= 0) {
   311	        abortWithMessage(rank, "no CUDA device is available");
   312	    }
   313	    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
   314	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));
   315	
   316	    omp_set_dynamic(0);
   317	
   318	    if (rank == 0) {
   319	        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   320	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
   321	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   322	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", worldSize);
   323	        std::printf("OpenMP threads per rank: %d\n", omp_get_max_threads());
   324	        std::printf("CUDA devices per node: %d\n", deviceCount);
   325	        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   326	    }
   327	
   328	    std::vector<double> localA(localElements);
   329	    std::vector<double> B(matrixElements);
   330	    initMatrixRows(localA, N, firstRow, localRows);
   331	
   332	    // B is identical on every rank.  Only rank zero initializes it; the
   333	    // chunked broadcast also supports matrices larger than MPI's int count.
   334	    if (rank == 0) {
   335	        initMatrix(B, N);
   336	    }
   337	    broadcastDoubles(B.data(), matrixElements, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   338	
   339	    double* deviceA = nullptr;
   340	    double* deviceB = nullptr;
   341	    double* deviceC = nullptr;
   342	    const std::size_t safeLocalElements = std::max<std::size_t>(localElements, 1);
   343	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA),
   344	                          safeLocalElements * sizeof(double)));
   345	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB),
   346	                          matrixElements * sizeof(double)));
   347	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC),
   348	                          safeLocalElements * sizeof(double)));
   349	
   350	    if (localElements > 0) {
   351	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, localA.data(),
   352	                              localElements * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   353	    }
   354	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceB, B.data(),
   355	                          matrixElements * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   356	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   357	
   358	    std::vector<double> localC(localElements);
   359	    const bool needFullResult = validate || printResults;
   360	    std::vector<double> C;
   361	    if (rank == 0 && needFullResult) {
   362	        C.resize(matrixElements);
   363	    }
   364	
   365	    if (rank == 0) {
   366	        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   367	    }
   368	
   369	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   370	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   371	
   372	    if (localRows > 0) {
   373	        const dim3 block(static_cast<unsigned int>(kTileColumns),
   374	                         static_cast<unsigned int>(kTileRows), 1);
   375	        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((N + kTileColumns - 1) / kTileColumns),
   376	                        static_cast<unsigned int>((localRows + kTileRows - 1) / kTileRows),
   377	                        1);
   378	        matrixMultiplyKernel<<<grid, block>>>(deviceA, deviceB, deviceC,
   379	                                              static_cast<int>(localRows),
   380	                                              static_cast<int>(N));
   381	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   382	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   383	    }
   384	
   385	    if (localElements > 0) {
   386	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(localC.data(), deviceC,
   387	                              localElements * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   388	    }
   389	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
   390	
   391	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   392	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   393	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   394	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   395	
   396	    if (needFullResult) {
   397	        if (localElements > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   398	            abortWithMessage(rank, "local matrix result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count capacity");
   399	        }
   400	
   401	        std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   402	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   403	        if (rank == 0) {
   404	            receiveCounts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize));
   405	            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(worldSize));
   406	            for (int source = 0; source < worldSize; ++source) {
   407	                std::size_t sourceFirstRow = 0;
   408	                std::size_t sourceRows = 0;
   409	                calculateRowPartition(N, worldSize, source,
   410	                                      sourceFirstRow, sourceRows);
   411	                const std::size_t sourceElements = sourceRows * N;
   412	                const std::size_t sourceDisplacement = sourceFirstRow * N;
   413	                if (sourceElements > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
   414	                    sourceDisplacement > static_cast<std::size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   415	                    abortWithMessage(rank, "matrix is too large for MPI_Gatherv metadata");
   416	                }
   417	                receiveCounts[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
   418	                    static_cast<int>(sourceElements);
   419	                displacements[static_cast<std::size_t>(source)] =
   420	                    static_cast<int>(sourceDisplacement);
   421	            }
   422	        }
   423	
   424	        MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localElements), MPI_DOUBLE,
   425	                    rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr,
   426	                    rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   427	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   428	                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceA));
   432	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceB));
   433	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceC));
   434	    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
   435	
   436	    if (rank == 0) {
   437	        const long durationMilliseconds = std::max<long>(
   438	            1L, static_cast<long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0)));
   439	        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
   440	
   441	        const double gflops = (2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
   442	                               static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N)) /
   443	                              (elapsed > 0.0 ? elapsed : 1e-12) / 1e9;
   444	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   445	
   446	        if (printResults) {
   447	            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
   448	        }
   449	
   450	        if (validate) {
   451	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   452	            std::vector<double> validationA(matrixElements);
   453	            initMatrix(validationA, N);
   454	            const bool valid = validateResult(validationA, B, C, N);
   455	            if (valid) {
   456	                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   457	            } else {
   458	                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   459	                MPI_Finalize();
   460	                return 1;
   461	            }
   462	        }
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    MPI_Finalize();
   466	    return 0;
   467	}
